Interpretation: the 5× target must be assessed from resident/full-job throughput; synthetic GEMM timings do not establish it. Saved capture score arrays are final candidate likelihood scores, not raw complex correlations; correlation-stage timing exists but paired correlation values do not. Holdout correctness is the correctly admitted bit/capture outcome; timing and epoch synchronization are not separate scored endpoints here.
